The finite-dimensional Lean model is exactly that class at a point: the (2,1) part is a real bilinear pairing A of the coframe bivector e∧e with the curvature R (both in Λ²ℝ^{1,3}, carrier BivectorIndex = Fin 2 × Fin 3, the spacetime two-form part fixed by the wedge) and the (4,0) part is an alternating coframe four-form ω. Lorentz naturality = invariance of A under the actual induced bivector action inducedBivectorAction Λ (2×2 minors) of every proper orthochronous Lorentz matrix (IsProperLorentz). Results: (i) lorentz_natural_operator_classification (eq:supp-lorentz-commutant): the commutant is span{I, ⋆} (only two rotations and one boost of cosh = 5/4 are used, so the hypothesis is weaker than full naturality); (ii) lorentz_natural_pairing_classification: every invariant pairing is a·(metric = Holst η-contraction) + b·(ε = Palatini ε-contraction), coefficients unique (pairing_coefficients_unique); (iii) alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det: every alternating four-form is λ·det (L_vol); (iv) gravitational_score_classification: the pair (A, ω) has a unique coefficient triple (α, β, λ), i.e. eq:main-phv-coefficients / eq:main-phv-classification. Scoped modelling disclosed: the manuscript hypotheses 'scalar residual typed multiplicity commutant' and 'represents determinant coframe volume' are the assumptions of the model (no extra internal tensor; the volume term is an alternating four-form of the coframe); the class's comparison modulo exact terms and the exclusion of torsion polynomials / curvature derivatives / inverse coframes are the model's shape (A is linear in R and quadratic in e, ω is quartic in e) rather than separately proved exclusions. No Palatini, Holst or Einstein action is assumed, and nothing is claimed for first-order connection actions outside the class, as the new final sentence states. |

First box eq:main-curvature-energy-propagation on a finite-dimensional real inner-product space with a differentiable symmetric positive Gram M(t): for the writer y' = (K+L)y + f with M-skew K (<M x, K x> = 0) and any growth function a >= 0 with <x, (M' + M L + L^T M) x> <= 2 a <x, M x> (the manuscript's a_X = (1/2) max{0, lambda_max[...]} is the least such function, so the theorem applies to it), z(t) <= exp(int_0^t a) [z(0) + int_0^t exp(-int_0^s a) \|f(s)\|_{M(s)} ds] for t >= 0 (curvature_energy_propagation): energy differential inequality (z^2)' <= 2 a z^2 + 2 z \|f\|_M via exact skew cancellation and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality of the positive form (gram_inner_le, discriminant argument), then the integrating-factor/monotonicity argument on sqrt(z^2 + eps^2) and eps -> 0 (a genuinely time-dependent Gronwall bound; Mathlib's gronwallBound is constant-coefficient). Budgets: z <= exp(A*)(Z* + F*) on [0,T*] (curvature_energy_uniform_bound). Second box eq:main-derived-l2-curvature (derived_l2_curvature_bound): with slice norms rho(t) = \|I y(t)\|_{L^2(Sigma_t)} <= C_I z(t), lapse 0 <= N <= N*, and \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int_0^{T*} N rho^2) + C_zeta, one gets \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_I sqrt(N* T*) exp(A*)(Z* + F*) + C_zeta. Scoped/disclosed: the writer hypotheses are stated for all real times (only [0,t] is used), y and M differentiable, f and a continuous; the slice decomposition of L^2(K) (\|g\|^2_{L^2(K)} = int N \|g(t)\|^2_{Sigma_t}), the triangle inequality in L^2(K) and \|zeta\|_{L^2(K)} <= C_zeta are abstracted into the single hypothesis \|R\|_{L^2(K)} <= sqrt(int N rho^2) + C_zeta. |

| `thm:main-finite-homogeneous-stationarity` | theorem | Finite stationary classification and de Sitter convergence | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.discreteAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.lapseEuler`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.scaleEuler`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.hasDerivAt_discreteAction_lapse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.hasDerivAt_discreteAction_scale`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.IsFiniteStationary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.IsPositivePath`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.SatisfiesRecurrence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.stationary_recurrence_of_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.stationary_of_recurrence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.stationary_const_of_B_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.no_positive_stationary_of_B_neg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.log_ratio_sub_two_mul_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.log_ratio_eq_two_artanh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.renewalTime`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.log_profile_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.scale_factor_log_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.density_log_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteHomogeneousStationarity.conductance_log_error_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/FiniteHomogeneousStationarityExact.lean) | Proved. Euler equations: the partial derivatives (HasDerivAt) of S_d = -sum_{j<M}[A_0 (q_{j+1}-q_j)^2/s_j + B_0 s_j qbar_j^2] in s_j (j<M) and in the interior node q_j (1<=j<M, endpoints fixed) are exactly eq:main-discrete-lapse-constraint and eq:main-discrete-scale-euler. Classification: for A_0,B_0>0, omega = sqrt(B_0/A_0), every positive stationary path (both Euler expressions vanish) has one common sign sigma in {+-1} with q_{j+1} = q_j (1+sigma omega s_j/2)/(1-sigma omega s_j/2) and 0<omega s_j/2<1; converse proved; Lambda=0 forces constant q, Lambda<0 has no positive stationary path. Convergence: with tau_j = sum_{i<j} s_i, T = sum_{j<M} s_j, s_j <= h_t, 0 <= omega h_t/2 <= b < 1: \|log(q_j/q_0) - sigma omega tau_j\| <= omega^3 T h_t^2/(12(1-b^2)) for all j<=M, via 0 <= log((1+x)/(1-x)) - 2x <= 2x^3/(3(1-b^2)) on [0,b] (= 2 artanh x remainder, proved by derivative monotonicity), and the induced (2/3, 2, 4/3)-scaled logarithmic errors for a_j = q_j^{2/3} (drift sigma H_0, H_0 = 2 omega/3), rho_j = q_j^2, kappa_j = q_j^{-4/3}. Scoped: sequences on N with indices j<=M; 0 <= b added (automatic when M>=1); the identification with the continuum profiles of cor:main-derived-desitter-rates is the displayed exponential bound (no separate limit statement). |

Geometry.OperationalActionMatching.geomLambda_eq_three_hubble_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/OperationalActionMatchingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalActionMatching.bulkAction_eq_discrete_rescaled`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/OperationalActionMatchingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalActionMatching.actionLambda_sub_geomLambda`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/OperationalActionMatchingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalActionMatching.einsteinTensor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/OperationalActionMatchingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalActionMatching.einstein_measured_mismatch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/OperationalActionMatchingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalActionMatching.einstein_matched_iff_rankDefect_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/OperationalActionMatchingExact.lean) | Proved. eq:main-observed-cost: E_s(x,y) = -eps log(Z_s Q_s/p_s) for finite alphabets F (Finset R) with positive proposal weights, eps != 0. eq:main-offshell-action-match: for every path with q_{j+1} in the stage alphabet the observed writer equals the bulk action identically (C terms telescope, Finset.sum_range_sub); the first-variation clause is read as this pointwise off-shell identity on the whole parametric domain (no separate derivative statement). Mismatch box: y -> E_s(x,y) is minimised at y = r(s) x with r(s) = (A - Bs^2/4)/(A + Bs^2/4 + Cs) (scoped: A,B,s>0, Bs^2 < 4A, C^2 <= AB), log r(s)/s -> c = -C/A as s -> 0 (HasDerivAt at s = 0), the n-step skeleton with step tau/n converges to q_0 e^{c tau}; Lambda_act = 4B/(3A) is the B_0/2 of the A/A_0-rescaled discrete action (A_0 = 8/3), Lambda_geom = 4C^2/(3A^2) = 3H^2 with H = 2c/3 (q = a^{3/2}); using RelationalDeSitterBranch.einstein_cosmological_vacuum for the de Sitter metric with Hubble rate H: G + Lambda_act g = (4 D/(3A^2)) g as 4x4 matrices, and the residual vanishes iff D = AB - C^2 = 0. |
| `thm:supp-right-congruence` | theorem | History-level right congruence | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.branch_eq_of_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.step_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.futureEquivalent_equivalence`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | Future equivalence is an equivalence; equivalent histories have equal one-letter branch probabilities (eq:supp-one-letter-equality) and, whenever branch h a != 0 (the positive-probability branch), h a ~ h' a. | `thm:supp-minimal-record` | theorem | Canonical minimum predictive realization | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.derived_predictive_relation_carrier`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_branch`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_update_of_occurs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.generated_ledger_is_compiled_presentation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | For every reachable deterministic realization there is a unique map pi : State -> Z^min with pi o stateOf = q (toMinimal_unique), it is surjective, intertwines updates on occurring branches (pi(u_a s) = T_a(pi s) when rate s a != 0) and branch functions (kappa_a o pi = r_a); hence any ledger/hidden-state presentation has a unique quotient onto Z^min, and two ledger states are identified iff all their future laws agree. Fibrewise in cut type. |
| `thm:supp-persistence-derived` | theorem | Persistence derived from composition | [`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.minimalUpdate_mk_of_occurs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.step_futureEquivalent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversal.PredictiveSystem.Realization.toMinimal_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/DerivedPredictiveCarrierUniversalExact.lean) | eq:supp-persistence is the instance minimalUpdate_mk_of_occurs with h = H_n and a = A_{n+1}: on any realized (positive-probability) branch [H_n A_{n+1}] = T_{A_{n+1}}([H_n]); the derived packet (Z_n, A_{n+1}, Z_{n+1}) is therefore a function of the event process, and every other deterministic persistent packet maps uniquely onto it by toMinimal_unique/surjective. Not stated as a separate named theorem in Lean; it is a direct instance of the cited results. |

Typing is by arbitrary index types per cut/state. |
| `thm:supp-comb-tomography` | theorem | Finite process-comb tomography | [`RenewalGeometry.reconstructChoiTensor_pairing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.reconstructChoiTensor_frame_independent`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.physicalChoiBranches_separate_hermitian`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsDeterministicCombThrough`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.linkedCombPurificationCertificate_exists`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finiteDeterministicComb_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.causalCombPrefixes_unique_from_terminal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.finiteCombPrefix_minimalPurification_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteProcessCombTomography.lean) | Re-verified against the new text. Positive trace-normalized physical probes separate Hermitian Choi tensors, so the tensor reconstructed from the frame probabilities is the unique Hermitian representative and is frame independent. The conditions R^(0) = 1, R^(k) >= 0 and the nested output-trace recursion (IsDeterministicCombThrough) construct canonical linked minimal-rank square-root factors obeying the same causal recursion; support minimality in the new sense (minimal Stinespring dilation of every prefix channel) is exactly the memory dimension finrank (CanonicalPrefixMemory J) = rank J of finiteCombPrefix_minimalPurification_unique, and any other prefix-support-minimal factor differs by a unique inner-product-preserving memory gauge (the unitary U_k). Scoped: the converse 'realization implies conditions' holds because FiniteDeterministicComb carries the conditions as a field; the link isometries V_k are represented by the linked factors rather than as explicit maps H_{2k-2} (x) A_{k-1} -> H_{2k-1} (x) A_k; the final memory discard, the transport of 'named operators retained in the compared record realization' by the same unitaries (a consequence of uniqueness of U_k, not a separate theorem) and the closing remark on the classical predictive quotient are not separately formalized. |

Finite matrix-block model realized with a C*-algebra structure. |

| Proved (new file Predictive/FlatWordPanelReconstruction.lean on top of WordGramPositivityExact/FlatWordReconstructionExact, compiled, standard axioms). flat_word_reconstruction is thm:supp-word-flatness in full, for a represented generator family gen on a nonempty finite carrier closed under a letter involution inv (star (gen g) = gen (inv g)): (i) every panel K_{X,r} is positive semidefinite; (ii) there is a least r_X with rank K_{X,r_X} = rank K_{X,r_X+1} (eq:supp-flat-stop, strict growth below); (iii) W_{X,<=r_X} = A^hist and the spans are constant from r_X on; (iv) the panel through depth r_X+1 reconstructs the object: for any represented family gen' over the same alphabet and involution on any nonempty finite carrier M' with gramPanel gen (r_X+1) = gramPanel gen' (r_X+1) there is a star-algebra isomorphism psi : A^hist ~=* A'^hist with psi [w] = [w]' for EVERY history word w (units, generator multiplication, involution and every represented mixed history), and psi matches the predictive core algebras StarAlgebra.adjoin (gen '' Delta) of every sub-alphabet Delta (in particular the minimum predictive state algebra). Construction (exists_starAlgEquiv_of_panel_eq): the word coordinate map T_d c = sum_v c_v [v] has kernel exactly the Gram kernel of the panel, c in ker T_d <-> c^H K_{X,d} c = 0 (mem_ker_wordCombination_iff, faithfulness of the regular trace), so equal panels give equal kernels; at a flat depth T_r and T_{r+1} are onto A^hist (exhaustion clause) and flatness transfers to gen' via the depth-r sub-panel (gramPanel_eq_submatrix); panelEquiv is the induced linear isomorphism (first isomorphism theorem, quotients by the common kernel); multiplicativity comes from the depth-(r+1) relation [g] sum_v c_v [v] = sum_v c_v [g v] (genElt_mul_wordCombination) plus Algebra.adjoin_induction; the involution from [v]^* = [reverse (map inv v)] (star_wordCombination). Disclosed rendering: 'reconstructs' = reconstruction up to a canonical star-isomorphism compatible with the word labels (the paper's proof: 'the one-step extension reconstructs the generator multiplication matrices and reversed words reconstruct the involution'). Scoped hypotheses: nonempty finite carriers, finite alphabet (Fintype (Words Gamma d)), the letter involution inv is part of the data (equivalent to the earlier star-closure hypothesis by choice). |

The new wording replaces 'introduces exactly one independent nondegenerate signed line' by 'satisfies def:main-signed-extension on exactly one independent line ℝt', i.e. a negative-definite form q_L on the line with the orthogonal extension q = q_L ⊕ g. Encoding: the positive spatial form g on W_4 is a positive definite Hermitian matrix D on Fin 3; the extension is the orthogonal block matrix oneLineSignedExtension c D on Fin 1 ⊕ Fin 3, and the negative-definite line form of the definition is c < 0 (so what was previously a disclosed reading of 'signed' is now literally the hypothesis of the definition). supp_signature_minimality: (a) the positive packet cannot supply a negative direction (quadForm D ≥ 0 everywhere, negInertia D = 0); (b) c = -a² for a unique a > 0, q(τt+w) = -a²τ² + g(w,w) (eq:supp-signature-inertia), inertia (neg,pos,null) = (1,3,0), the overall-sign reversal -q has (3,1,0), and Sylvester's law makes the count invariant under every invertible change of basis X ↦ X^H q X. No gamma matrices are used. Disclosed: the independence of the protected orientation row from the positive packet (thm:supp-relative-primitive-floor) enters only as the hypothesis that the line is adjoined orthogonally/independently. |

| `thm:supp-action-stationarity` | theorem | Quantitative accepted-action stationarity | [`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.integral_bregman_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.integral_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.integral_stationarity_form_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.vanishing_gap_forces_stationarity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.resid_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.grad_action_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean) | Kernel-integrated conclusions for a stationary law K∘ν = ν of the accepted kernel: ∫D_Ψ ≤ η Δ̄, ∫‖y-θ‖^2 ≤ (2η/m) Δ̄, and ∫S dν ≤ (4Λ_Φ/m + 4M^2(1+ηℓ)^2/(m^2 η)) Δ̄ for every 0 ≤ S ≤ m^{-1}‖∇A‖^2 (the G^{-1} ⪯ m^{-1}I rendering of the stationarity form); the Lean constant 4Λ_Φ/m is ≤ the paper's 4Λ_Φ^2/(m μ_Φ) because Λ_Φ ≥ μ_Φ, so the paper's admissible constant follows. Scoped: the Hessian bounds are rendered by gradient monotonicity/Lipschitz inequalities, y*_θ is assumed a global minimizer of Φ_θ, and integrability of A and of the gap under ν⊗K is assumed. |
| `prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity` | proposition | Cutoff-aware stationarity on the determining test core | [`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.physical_test_stationarity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.inner_sq_le_of_gram_budget`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_meanSquare_of_scaled_gap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.exists_subseq_ae_tendsto_of_meanSquare`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.exists_subseq_ae_tendsto_of_integral_sq_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.exists_strictMono_diagonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_zero_of_tendsto_ofReal_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.physical_test_stationarity_core`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/PhysicalTestStationarityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.BregmanStationarity.integral_stationarity_form_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact.lean) | Proved as three theorems matching the three claims. (1) eq:main-physical-stationarity (physical_test_stationarity): in the chart setting of thm:supp-action-stationarity (AcceptedBregmanStationarityExact: Fisher potential, common action, accepted Markov kernel with stationary probability law nu, all the chart constants), with the positive chart Gram G(theta) >= m I and a represented test lift v with budget <v,G v> <= b = B_{X,K}(k)^2 (eq:main-test-lift-budget; the sup over supp nu is encoded as a bound at every field value) and measurable first variation <grad A, v>, one gets E_nu <grad A(theta), v(theta)>^2 <= b * C_X * Delta-bar with C_X the boxed constant of integral_stationarity_form_le; the duality step is inner_sq_le_of_gram_budget (\|<g,v>\|^2 <= m^{-1}\|g\|^2 <v,Gv>). (2) mean-square convergence on the core under eq:supp-scaled-test-gap: tendsto_meanSquare_of_scaled_gap (squeeze). (3) common coupling: on one measure space carrying the cutoff variables Y X m (cutoff X : N, core test m : N) with E[(Y X m)^2] <= b X m -> 0 in X for every m, exists_subseq_ae_tendsto_of_meanSquare / _of_integral_sq_le produce a strictly increasing deterministic cutoff sequence phi along which a.e. every Y (phi j) m -> 0; proof = the paper's diagonal choice b(phi j, m) <= 2^-j for m <= j (exists_strictMono_diagonal), Tonelli (lintegral_tsum) + ae_lt_top' for the a.s. finite sum, terms -> 0, and ae_all_iff over the countable core; no independence used. physical_test_stationarity_core bundles (2)+(3). Disclosed: (1) and (2)-(3) are separate theorems (the coupling is abstracted as second-moment bounds b X m, which (1) supplies cutoff by cutoff); the remark on extending zero from the core to all determining tests by uniform continuity is not formalised (it is not part of the statement). |

| `thm:supp-face-linearization` | theorem | Derivative-free renewal face linearization | [`RenewalGeometry.renewal_face_linearization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/FaceLinearization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.additive_locallyBounded_toContinuousLinearMap`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/StableRenewalFaceLinearization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LocalizedRenewalFaceLinearizationExact.localized_renewal_face_linearization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/LocalizedRenewalFaceLinearizationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.stable_renewal_face_linearization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/StableRenewalFaceLinearization.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.stableRenewalFaceLimit`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/StableRenewalFaceLinearization.lean) | Exact branch: halving symmetry plus quasi-additivity kill the additivity defect (2^{-n} scaling) so A is additive; additivity plus boundedness near 0 gives a unique continuous real-linear functional, and the localized version does this on a halving-closed neighbourhood U (agreement on U). Stable branch: summable adjacent defects \|L_{m+1}z - L_m z\| ≤ ε_m‖z‖ and additivity defects η_m → 0 give a unique continuous linear limit with pointwise convergence that is uniform on norm-bounded sets. | `prop:supp-restricted-classification` | proposition | Classification in the prescribed incidence and degree class | [`RenewalGeometry.lorentz_natural_pairing_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.pairing_coefficients_unique`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.gravitational_score_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.inducedBivectorAction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsProperLorentz`](../../RenewalGeometry/Lorentz/LorentzNaturalBivectorClassificationExact.lean) | Proved in the same finite-dimensional model as thm:main-gravitational-classification (this proposition is its core classification clause, stated within def:main-gravitational-class). An element of the class is modelled pointwise as a real bilinear pairing A of the coframe bivector with the curvature (the bidegree-(2,1) part, carrier Fin 2 × Fin 3) plus an alternating coframe four-form ω (the degree-four part). Lorentz naturality = invariance of A under the induced bivector action of every proper orthochronous Lorentz matrix. lorentz_natural_pairing_classification: A = a·metricPairing + b·epsilonPairing, i.e. a·L_Holst + b·L_Palatini with unique coefficients; alternating_four_form_eq_smul_det: ω = λ·det = λ·L_vol; gravitational_score_classification packages the unique triple. The connection enters only through R, so nothing about torsion is assumed: the classification holds before torsion is set to zero, as the statement says. Scoped modelling disclosed: 'scalar multiplicity commutant' and 'represented determinant volume' are the model's assumptions; comparison modulo exact terms is not modelled at the pointwise algebraic level. |

Scoped: the identification of the Euler source with P_{α,β} K_e(T) enters as a hypothesis (hCartanStationary), and injectivity is passed to the packet as a hypothesis discharged by cartan_injective. |

stantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.tendsto_inv_atTop_of_tendsto_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/EfficientCutConstantScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteWeightedGraph.renewalSpatialPositiveScreen`](../../RenewalGeometry/Renewal/RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen.lean) | Proved on a finite weighted graph (masses m_v > 0, symmetric nonnegative conductances c, cell scale h > 0, efficient capacities u). The cut constants of eq:main-cut-constant are encoded as cutConstant w = sInf of the finite family of half-volume cut ratios w(∂A)/μ(A)^{2/3} (efficientCutConstant u = I^eff, spatialCutConstant h = I^sp with weights h c; the infimum is attained, exists_minimizing_cut, and equals 0 when no half-volume cut exists). cutConstant_comparison derives a₋ I^sp ≤ I^eff ≤ a₊ I^sp (eq:main-cut-comparison) from the edgewise comparison a₋ h c ≤ u ≤ a₊ h c; spatialCutConstant_ge_of_efficient gives I^sp ≥ I_*/a₊ from I^eff ≥ I_*; spatialCutMargin_of_le converts I^sp ≥ I into the two-sided cut margin I·min(μ(A), μ(Aᶜ))^{2/3} ≤ h c(∂A) for every A (eq:supp-cut-isoperimetry, complement case by cut-capacity symmetry). The main theorem renewalSpatialPositiveScreen_of_efficientCut then assembles, under μ(V) ≤ V_*, the weighted degree bound h² Σ_u c_uv ≤ D m_v, the comparison and I^eff ≥ I_* > 0, the full RenewalSpatialPositiveScreen package with constant I_*/a₊: L⁶ Sobolev (C_S = 128 D/(I_*/a₊)²), Poincaré (C_P = V_*^{2/3} C_S), positive spectral floor C_P⁻¹, Weyl count N(R) ≤ 1 + 4 e^{3/2} V_* (C_S R)^{3/2}, and the compact-screen tail Σ_{λ_j > R} \|⟨φ_j, f⟩\|² ≤ R⁻¹ 𝓔(f) (eq:supp-screen-count-tail, the spectral-coefficient form of ‖(I − P_R) f‖² ≤ R⁻¹ 𝓔(f)). Neck obstruction: neck_witness shows that for a half-volume cut of positive capacity the potential φ = 1_A/u(∂A) has ordered-pair total variation (1/2)ΣΣ u \|dφ\| = 1 and pairs with the demand b_A of eq:main-cut-demand to μ(A)^{2/3}/u(∂A); neck_witness_of_minimizing_cut gives exactly (I^eff)⁻¹ for a minimizing cut, and tendsto_inv_atTop_of_tendsto_zero sends it to +∞ along any family with I^eff → 0⁺ (eq:main-neck-obstruction). The 'zero-capacity cut retained as disconnected support' clause is a definitional remark and is not formalized separately. Scoped hypotheses: 0 ≤ a₋, 0 < a₊, 0 < D, V_*, h. |

Setting: a finite measure space (K, mu) (the compact cylinder with its finite reconstructed volume measure), a sequence of cutoffs e_n : K -> E (E a normed group; a real normed space for the bivector clause), the limit e, Mathlib eLpNorm' norms with real exponents. coframe_interpolation: from eq:supp-coframe-l2-l6 (\|\|e_n - e\|\|_2 -> 0, sup_n \|\|e_n\|\|_6 <= M_6 < infinity) one gets (i) e in L^6 with \|\|e\|\|_6 <= M_6 (eLpNorm'_limit_le_of_tendsto_L2: instead of weak compactness, convergence in measure gives an a.e.-convergent subsequence and Fatou is applied to \|e_n\|^6); (ii) eq:supp-interpolation-explicit \|\|e_n - e\|\|_q <= \|\|e_n - e\|\|_2^{theta_q} \|\|e_n - e\|\|_6^{1 - theta_q}, theta_q = 3/q - 1/2, for 2 <= q <= 6 (coframe_interpolation_explicit, from the existing Holder interpolation); (iii) eq:supp-strong-lq: \|\|e_n - e\|\|_q -> 0 for every 2 <= q < 6 (coframe_strong_Lq, using \|\|e_n - e\|\|_6 <= 2 M_6 and theta_q > 0); (iv) for p > 3/2 and p' = p/(p-1): 2 < 2p' < 6 and, for every bounded bilinear map B (the exterior product on the finite-dimensional coframe fibres), B(e_n, e_n) -> B(e, e) strongly in L^{p'} (coframe_bivector_strong: splitting B(e_n,e_n) - B(e,e) = B(e_n - e, e_n) + B(e, e_n - e), Holder with 1/p' = 1/(2p') + 1/(2p') via eLpNorm'_bilinear_le, uniform L^{2p'} bounds from the L^6 bounds on the finite measure, and (iii) at q = 2p'). Scoped hypotheses disclosed: cutoffs indexed by N (a sequence), all functions AEStronglyMeasurable, M_6 given as an extended real with M_6 < infinity. |

| Proved on the odd periodic grid (ℤ/N)³, N = 2m+1, for grid functions with values in any seminormed group (weighted forms (1/N)Σ‖u(x+r)−u(x)‖² = ‖(S_r−I)u/h‖_h²). connector_coercivity: (1/5)𝒟_C ≤ 𝒟_+ ≤ 9𝒟_C (eq:supp-open-connector-coercivity) via S_{e2}−I = S_{e1}^{-1}[(S_{e1+e2}−I)−(S_{e1}−I)], shift invariance and ‖a+b‖² ≤ 2‖a‖²+2‖b‖². root_graph_distance: every root path (sequence of A3 roots The writer eq:main-open-writer is defined for grid functions on an arbitrary additive group of sites with step vectors s_i and mesh h (fwd/bwd/ctr = D_i^±, D_i^0 of eq:main-open-differences; skewTransport = 𝖪_b; compensator = 𝖦_h with abstract coefficient derivatives Dc, Db; writerRhs / writerAcceleration = second row, a(g) v_t = Σ D_i^-(c^{ij}(g) D_j^+ q) − 𝖪_b v + 𝖦_h); the periodic grid (s_i = e_i, h = 1/N) and the lattice hℤ³ ⊂ ℝ³ (s_i = h e_i) are instances. Locality (writerRhs_local, writerAcceleration_local): the acceleration at x depends only on q, v at x and the twelve stencil neighbours x ± s_i, x − s_i + s_j (i ≠ j; InStencil). Two hops (grid_stencil_within_two_hops): on (ℤ/N)³ every stencil neighbour is x + d with d a sum of at most two steps of the execution graph of lem:supp-open-parity (A3 roots ∪ {±e_1}; ReachTwo, using IsRoot/ε/toGrid of RootParityConnectorExact). Continuum consistency (writer_continuum_consistency): for q ∈ C², v ∈ C¹, C¹ coefficients b^i, c^{ij}, continuous first-jet source F, with Dc^{ij}, Db^i the Fréchet derivatives, the right-hand side of the second row at x ∈ ℝ³ with steps h e_i tends as h ↓ 0 to Σ c^{ij}(g) D²q(x)(e_i)(e_j) − 2 Σ b^i(g) Dv(x)e_i + F(g,(v, Dq(x)e_·)), i.e. the harmonic normal row eq:main-harmonic-normal-row a q_tt = c^{ij}∂_i∂_j q − 2b^i∂_i q_t + F; the divergence contributes (∂_i c^{ij})∂_j q and the skew transport (∂_i b^i) v, cancelled exactly by the compensator via the chain rule (tendsto_flux_term, tendsto_skewTransport, tendsto_compensator); mixed second differences via Convex.taylor_approx_two_segment. Scoped: consistency is pointwise for the lattice writer on ℝ³ (same formula as the periodic grid for N-periodic smooth fields sampled on it); regularity q ∈ C², v, b, c ∈ C¹. |

New file Gravity/SupplementLawMultiplierOrders.lean proves the four order bounds eq:supp-law-orders directly in physical space for the paper's own forward-difference Sobolev norms eq:supp-open-norms (diffSobolevNorm: \|\|u\|\|_{r,h}^2 = sum_{\|alpha\|<=r} \|\|D^alpha u\|\|_h^2 with D^alpha = prod (D_i^+)^{alpha_i}, multiDiff over the multi-index set multiIndices r), no Fourier transform or norm equivalence needed: all forward/backward differences commute and Lambda_h commutes with D^alpha (gridLaplacian_multiDiff), D_i^- is a translate of D_i^+ so \|\|Lambda_h w\|\|^2 <= 3 sum_i \|\|D_i D_i w\|\|^2 without losing h (gridNormSq_gridLaplacian_le), and the already-proved domination eq:supp-law-domination supplies the h-losing steps; per array this gives h^4\|\|Lambda^2 w\|\|^2 <= 144 sum_{ij}\|\|D_j D_i w\|\|^2, <= 36 h^2 sum \|\|D_j D_j D_i w\|\|^2, <= 9 h^4 sum \|\|D_j D_j D_i D_i w\|\|^2 (biharmonic_bound_one/two/three), applied to w = D^alpha q. law_multiplier_bounds (periodic grid Fin 3 -> ZMod N, arrays valued in any real normed space) bundles eq:supp-law-domination for every array w with the four bounds h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-1,h} <= C_s \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-2,h} <= C_s h \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 q\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C_s h^2 \|\|q\|\|_{s+1,h}, h^2\|\|Lambda_h^2 v\|\|_{s-3,h} <= C_s h \|\|v\|\|_{s,h} with explicit h- and N-independent constants C_s = 36 sqrt(N_{s-1}), 18 sqrt(N_{s-2}), 9 sqrt(N_{s-3}), 18 sqrt(N_{s-3}) (N_r = #{alpha : \|alpha\| <= r}); exists_uniform_order_constants gives the existential 'C_s independent of h' form. Scoped hypotheses disclosed: h > 0, integer index s >= 3 so that s-1, s-2, s-3 are genuine (the paper applies the lemma with s >= 11; for s < 3 the truncated indices would make the h-gaining bounds false). The Fourier-side symbol facts eq:supp-law-laplacian remain in the earlier file (symbol_bounds, fourth_order_bounds) but are no longer needed for the lemma. |
| `prop:supp-law-switching` | proposition | Rapid switching is not included | [`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorSolution`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorVelocity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorSolution_hasDerivAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.oscillatorVelocity_hasDerivAt`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.quarterPeriodTransfer`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.quarterPeriodTransfer_mulVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.quarterPeriodTransfer_two_step`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.two_step_hasEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.two_step_hasEigenvalue_reciprocal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.cornerFrequencySq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.corner_frequency_ratio_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.corner_frequency_ratio_ne_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.corner_frequency_sqrt_ratio_ne_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.two_step_pow_entry_abs`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.switching_modulus_gt_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.switching_amplification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RapidLawSwitching.lean) | Proved as the explicit finite statement the proposition makes about the scalar corner mode. The oscillator q'' = -Omega^2 q with data (q0,p0) is the explicit trajectory oscillatorSolution (verified by HasDerivAt); its quarter-period state map is the matrix [[0,1/Omega],[-Omega,0]] (quarterPeriodTransfer_mulVec); the two-law product is diag(-Omega_1/Omega_2, -Omega_2/Omega_1) with those eigenvalues (Module.End.HasEigenvalue of toLin'); with the dispersion eq:supp-law-dispersion Omega^2 = l(1 + sigma h^2 l) at the corner h^2 l = 12 the ratio Omega_1^2/Omega_2^2 = (1+12 sigma_1)/(1+12 sigma_2) != 1 for sigma_1 != sigma_2 in the admissible interval 1+12 sigma_i > 0; after n switching cycles the diagonal entries have modulus (Omega_1/Omega_2)^{+-n} and with n >= T/(K h) cycles of duration <= K h this is >= exp((log rho / K) T/h), rho = max ratio > 1. Scoping disclosed: the identification of the resolved corner mode with a scalar oscillator (the paper's first sentence of the proof) is the modelling input, and the transfer matrix is defined from the explicit solution rather than through ODE uniqueness. |
| `thm:supp-law-row-neighbourhood` | theorem | A chronological law neighborhood with the same Einstein limit | [`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.expect_totalCost_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.prob_cost_gt_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.WordProcess.prob_lt_le_expect_div`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.odd_cutoff_eventually_not_exceptional`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.isOpen_rowConstraintSet_restrict`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.convex_rowConstraintSet_inter`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.rowConstraintSet_nonempty`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ChronologicalRow.rowConstraintSet_exists_pos_row`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean) | Finite chronological word process (WordProcess: arbitrary rows depending on the whole prefix, rows nonnegative summing to one; stopped prefixes carry zero cost, so the per-prefix bound is a constraint at live prefixes only). Boxed content: expect_totalCost_le gives E C_h <= M T h^4 from sum_e K_{h,j}(e\|pi) c_{h,j}(pi,e) <= M tau_j h^4 at every prefix of stage j < stages and sum_j tau_j <= T (conditioning on live prefixes = tower recursion, no dependence on reaching probabilities); prob_cost_gt_le gives P{C_h > h^2} <= M T h^2 via Markov (prob_lt_le_expect_div). Borel-Cantelli: odd_cutoff_eventually_not_exceptional shows that for ANY measure mu (common coupling) with mu(A_m) <= K/(2m+1)^2 the events A_m occur only finitely often a.e. Openness: rowConstraintSet (strict conditional-mean inequalities on a finite product of rows) is open, convex, relatively open in the product of stdSimplex rows (isOpen_rowConstraintSet_restrict), convex inside the product of simplexes, nonempty when a letter of cost below threshold exists at each source (Dirac rows), and contains strictly positive rows (small uniform mixing). Scoping: the paper's strict '<' is weakened to '<=' in the hypothesis (stronger result); the clause 'on {C_h <= h^2} the history obeys lem:supp-law-cost-control with d_h = h and has the same Einstein limit' is a citation of lem:supp-law-cost-control / thm:supp-law-einstein and is not restated in Lean. |
| `prop:supp-law-transport` | proposition | Physical profile transport certifies a row neighborhood | [`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.W2`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.IsCoupling`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.weighted_sqrt_sum_add_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.sqrt_row_cost_le_sqrt_add_W2`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.row_moment_of_W2_lt`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.W2_le_sqrt_cost_add_totalVariation`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProfileTransport.isOpen_W2_ball_restrict`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/SupplementLawRowNeighbourhoodExact.lean) | W2 d K P is defined as the infimum over couplings (nonnegative pi with marginals K, P) of sqrt(sum pi d^2), profiles Phi e in an arbitrary normed group (the paper's L^2 profile Hilbert space is an instance), c_e = \|\|Phi e\|\|^2, d = \|\|Phi e - Phi e'\|\|. sqrt_row_cost_le_sqrt_add_W2 is eq:supp-law-transport-bound for all rows K, P in the simplex (Minkowski in weighted l^2 under each coupling, then infimum; no minimiser needed). row_moment_of_W2_lt: sum P c <= M0 tau h^4 and W2 < gamma sqrt(tau) h^2 give sum K c < M tau h^4 for every M > (sqrt M0 + gamma)^2 (tau, h > 0). Relative openness of the strict balls {K in simplex : W2(K,P) < rho}: proved (isOpen_W2_ball_restrict) via an explicit coupling perturbation giving the upper-semicontinuity bound W2(K',P)^2 <= cost(pi0) + D * TV(K',K0) for every coupling pi0 of (K0,P) and D >= max d^2. No compactness argument is needed. |

| Genuine odd-grid computation on Fin 3 -> Fin N, N >= 3: roots-of-unity sums (sum_{a<N} e^{2 pi i m a/N} = 0 for N not dividing m, hence sum sin^2(2 pi a/N) = N/2 and the 3D orthogonality of the unit modes), explicit T_i (off-diagonal 1/sqrt2 entries) with frob(T_i,T_j) = delta_ij, frob(I,T_i) = 0, tr T_i = 0, the seed u_* = sum T_i cos(2 pi x_i), p = -(2/3) kappa I + sum b_i T_i sin(2 pi x_i), the quadratic Hamiltonian H^{[2]}(u,pi;N,beta) = N(\|\|pi\|\|_h^2 - 1/2\|\|tr pi\|\|_h^2 + 1/4 sum \|\|delta_i u\|\|_h^2) + sum beta^i <pi, delta_i u>_h exactly as displayed in the paper's proof (affine in lapse/shift, quadHam_eq_affine), and meanJet_seed = eq:supp-initial-quadratic-mean: Q_h(theta) = (2/3 kappa^2 - 1/2 sum b_i^2 - 3/8 omega_h^2, -omega_h b_i/2). Root theta* = (3 omega_h/4, 0) and the second branch -(3 omega_h/4); Jacobian diag(omega_h, -omega_h/2, -omega_h/2, -omega_h/2) proved as HasFDerivAt (Qh_hasFDerivAt) plus the exact quadratic expansion; omega_h = 2h^{-1} sin(pi h) >= 4 for h <= 1/2 and the inverse Jacobian has entries bounded by 1/2 (uniform invertibility). Scoping disclosed: (i) H^{[2]} is taken as the definition (the paper's assertion that it is the quadratic jet of the full retained action, with cubic link remainder, is the input of its proof and is not formalised); (ii) the odd phase derivative delta_i enters through its symbol action on the unit modes, packaged as the hypothesis delta_i u_* = -omega_h sin(2 pi x_i) T_i (this is the definition eq:supp-initial-phase-derivative evaluated on the unit modes; the DFT is not formalised). |
| `thm:supp-exact-constraint-rank` | theorem | Finite characteristic quotient on a regular one-null branch | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.bracket_mul_lambda_eq_source`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.ker_diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_ker_diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_range_diracMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.finrank_range_diracMatrix_of_dim`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactConstraintRank.characteristic_quotient_dim`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/SupplementExactConstraintRankExact.lean) | Abstract block-matrix formulation with the paper's objects as parameters: Lambda a finite-dimensional real multiplier space, M = D_lambda P_h, A = the constraint bracket, B = the consistency source, lambda the multiplier. diracMatrix M A (r,s) = (-M s, M r + A s) is eq:supp-exact-dirac-matrix. Under ker M = span{lambda}, lambda != 0, A lambda = B (the homogeneity identity; bracket_mul_lambda_eq_source records its derivation D_X P (J (D_X P)^* lambda) = D_X P F^can once F^can = J (D_X P)^* lambda) and solvability of the first consistency condition (some r0 with M r0 = B, e.g. r0 = M^dagger B), ker_diracMatrix gives ker D = span{(lambda,0), (-r0, lambda)}, finrank_ker = 2, rank D = 2 dim Lambda - 2 = 8V - 2 for dim Lambda = 4V, and the characteristic-quotient count 20V - 8V - 2 = 12V - 2. Disclosed gap: the identification of M, A, B with derivatives of the actual reconstructed finite action, and the count of 8V independent constraints from 'D_X P_h onto', are not formalised (they enter as the parameters/arithmetic), exactly as in the paper's proof which uses only the listed algebraic facts. |

New file Gravity/SupplementExactTransverseElimination.lean: (1) abstract elimination for linear maps A : E -> H, G : Y -> H into a finite-dimensional complex inner product space: Pi_A = orthogonal projection onto range A (Mathlib starProjection), Gbar = (I - Pi_A) G (residual), C = A^{-1} Pi_A G for injective A (elimCoeff), U(e,y) = (e - C y, y) a linear automorphism (blockUnit); F U = [A Gbar] (coprod_comp_blockUnit), range [A Gbar] = range F (range_coprod_residual: the column transformation leaves the actual target unchanged), rank F = rank A + rank Gbar (finrank_range_coprod, eq:supp-exact-rank-split) and for symmetric K with K A = A/2 the congruence <F U x, K F U x'> = 1/2 <A e, A e'> + <Gbar y, K Gbar y'> (gram_blockUnit, eq:supp-exact-Gram-split). (2) Instantiation: toEuc identifies mode families K -> Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 3) C with EuclideanSpace C (K x Fin 3 x Fin 3), whose inner product is the summed Frobenius pairing (inner_toEuc); transverseSource = A_h restricted to the transverse space; signedGramMap = K0^{-1} = 1/2 I - 3/4 P_tr mode-wise, proved symmetric (signedGramMap_isSymmetric) and acting by 1/2 on the trace-free range of A_h (signedGramMap_transverseSource); rank A_h = 2\|K\| transported from the parent (finrank_range_transverseSource). transverse_elimination states, for every remaining source block G_h : Y -> H: rank F_h = 2\|K\| + rank Gbar_h, F_h U = [A_h Gbar_h] with equal range, and the signed-Gram congruence. Scoping inherited from the parent only: grid operators represented by their Fourier modes, \|K\| = V - 1 nonzero modes, so 2\|K\| = 2(V-1); the remaining source G_h is an arbitrary linear map (the paper does not specify it beyond 'the complete leading graded source'). |
| `thm:supp-exact-scalar-reconstruction` | theorem | Exact scalar--trace response reconstruction | [`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dewittK`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dewittKinv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.dewittK_mul_dewittKinv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.sourceProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.sourceResponse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceCompression`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceOp`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceSource`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.traceResponseVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.signedGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.reconstructed`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.IsResponse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.signedGram_isUnit_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.response_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction.reconstructed_isResponse`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ExactScalarTraceReconstruction.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ExactScalarReconstruction Setting: physical space n -> R, full-rank source F : Matrix n m R (Gram F^T F invertible, hypothesis hG) with target b, trace isometry E : Matrix n k R with E^T E = 1 (the paper's E tau = tau I/sqrt3), P_tr = E E^T, DeWitt K0 = 2(I - 3 P_tr) with K0^{-1} = (2I - 3P_tr)/4 (dewittK_mul_dewittKinv). Objects Pi = F (F^T F)^{-1} F^T, w = F(F^T F)^{-1} b, C = E^T Pi E, H = 2I - 3C, zeta = E^T w, chi = H^{-1} zeta (Matrix.inv), signed Gram F^T K0^{-1} F. signedGram_isUnit_iff: signed Gram invertible iff H invertible (via Sylvester det(1 - AB) = det(1 - BA); the explicit determinant identity signedGram_det). response_eq (eq:supp-exact-scalar-v): every v with F^T v = b and K0 v in Ran F (IsResponse) satisfies E^T v = -chi and v = w - 3(I - Pi) E chi; reconstructed_isResponse gives existence. dotProduct_reconstructed (eq:supp-exact-scalar-norm): \|v\|^2 = \|w\|^2 + 9 <chi,(I - C) chi>. response_tendsto_zero_iff (eq:supp-exact-response-equivalence): along any filter-indexed family with index-dependent dimension types, \|v_h\|^2 -> 0 iff \|w_h\|^2 -> 0 and \|H_h^{-1} E^* w_h\|^2 -> 0 (from the pointwise bounds response_norm_bounds). Scoped hypotheses: H invertible (regular signed Gram) is the hypothesis hH; the response is characterized by F^T v = b and K0 v in Ran F as in the paper's proof. |

the recursion traceOperator_mul_iteratedDeriv_traceResponse comes from Mathlib's Leibniz rule iteratedDeriv_mul with H^{(l)} = -3 C^{(l)}). hierarchy_eq_neg_mul_iteratedDeriv: if E^* v = -chi at every time then chi_j = -E^* v^{(j)}. deriv_traceResponse_eq and hasDerivAt_response are the two formulas of eq:supp-exact-first-time-source (chi' = H^{-1}(zeta' + 3 C' chi); v = w - 3(1-Pi)E chi gives v' = w' + 3 Pi' E chi - 3 (1-Pi) E chi'). Disclosed reformulation: the paper's vectors zeta, chi in R^V are formalized as elements of the same algebra as H, C (the recursion is linear and acts by left multiplication, so the vector statement is the column-wise special case, e.g. via Matrix.replicateCol); the explicit vector-valued corollary is not derived. |

eShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.lapse_shift_clock_condition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftClockTest.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.sumSq_le_of_coercive`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftFirstJetShadowing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.first_jet_shadowing_lapse_shift_rate`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftFirstJetShadowing.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.clock_test_of_first_jet_shadowing`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/LapseShiftFirstJetShadowing.lean) | Proved (new file Gravity/ExactMultiplierGradingClockTest.lean, compiled, standard axioms). exact_clock_test is prop:supp-exact-clock-test: along a regulator family i -> a(i) != 0, if at every site x of a finite site set V the exact-action ADM metric (N,beta,gamma) shadows a small harmonic ADM metric (N',beta', same gamma) in the initial first time derivatives of g_00 and g_0i (differences -> 0), with the same lapse N = 1, shift beta = 0 and spatial metric at the initial cut, uniformly coercive spatial metrics and a small harmonic first jet, then for every active/weak amplitude grading G of the 4V-dimensional multiplier space, with the multiplier rate lambda_t = lapseShiftJet (d_t N, d_t beta) and the normalized source coefficient u_a = S_a lambda_t, one has a^{-1} \|\|(u_a)_A\|\| -> 0 and a^{-2} \|\|(u_a)_W\|\| -> 0 (eq:supp-exact-rate-necessary); the electric coefficient does not enter. The two former interface hypotheses are discharged: (i) AmplitudeGrading Lambda is an orthogonal active/weak decomposition (weak subspace W with orthogonal projection, active = W-perp), scale a = S_a = diag(a I_A, a^2 I_W), normalizedSource a lambda_t = S_a lambda_t, and eq:supp-exact-rate-norm \|\|lambda_t\|\|^2 = a^{-2}\|\|(u_a)_A\|\|^2 + a^{-4}\|\|(u_a)_W\|\|^2 is the theorem AmplitudeGrading.rate_norm (Pythagoras norm_sq_eq + (u_a)_A = a lambda_A, (u_a)_W = a^2 lambda_W); (ii) the multiplier rate is the lapse-shift first jet (the ADM multipliers are lapse and shift), lapseShiftJet in EuclideanSpace R (V x Fin 4) with \|\|lambda_t\|\|^2 = sum_x ((d_t N_x)^2 + sum_j (d_t beta_x^j)^2) (lapseShiftJet_norm_sq); the proof composes the per-site geometric step first_jet_shadowing_lapse_shift_rate (ADM identities d_t g_00 = -2 d_t N, d_t g_0i = gamma_ij d_t beta^j + Cauchy-Schwarz) with lapse_shift_clock_condition. Disclosed renderings: 'multiplier solve' is encoded by its output relation lambda_t = S_a^{-1} u_a (equivalently u_a := S_a lambda_t), the only property of u_a the paper uses; the grading is an arbitrary orthogonal active/weak decomposition of the site multiplier space (the paper does not specify it further); 'small harmonic metric' = small lapse-shift first jet with a uniformly coercive shared spatial metric; the shadowing topology = initial first time derivative of g_00, g_0i at each site; a(i) != 0 along the family (the paper's a(h) -> 0 is not needed for the necessity statement). |

| `prop:supp-three-costs` | proposition | Three-cost identification and rank defect | [`RenewalGeometry.measuredQuadraticCost`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rankDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rankDefect_eq_det`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rankDefect_nonneg_of_posSemidef`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.measuredQuadraticCost_identify_B`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.measuredQuadraticCost_identify_C`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.measuredQuadraticCost_identify_A`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.measuredQuadraticCost_three_cost_identification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.measuredQuadraticCost_completion`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rankDefect_eq_zero_iff_rankOne`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.completion_second_term_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/ThreeCostIdentification.lean) | Proved. measuredQuadraticCost A B C s x y = A(y-x)^2/s + B s ((x+y)/2)^2 + C(y^2-x^2) (eq:main-measured-quadratic-cost). measuredQuadraticCost_three_cost_identification: for s > 0 and distinct u, v > 0 the three boxed formulas of eq:supp-three-cost-identification hold (B from E(u,u), C from the reversal difference, A from the reversal sum). measuredQuadraticCost_completion: eq:supp-cost-completion with c = -C/A and nu = D/A for A != 0, s != 0. rankDefect D = AB - | `lem:supp-entropy-energy` | lemma | History-level entropy--energy estimate | [`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.prevState`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.markovLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.markovLaw_sum_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.stageZ`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.acceptedRow`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.acceptedPathLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.pathCost`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.log_acceptedPathLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.finiteKL_acceptedPathLaw`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.neg_log_stageZ_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoryEntropyEnergy.entropy_energy_estimate`](../../RenewalGeometry/StatMech/HistoryEntropyEnergyEstimate.lean) | Proved at the path level. Finite horizon M, finite state space X, fixed initial value x0; paths are Fin M -> X. The accepted rows Q_j(x,.) = p_j(x,.) exp(-E_j(x,.)/eps)/Z_j(x) reuse gibbsRow/gibbsPartition of Action/FiniteGibbsActionGap.lean; Q is their Markov product law (acceptedPathLaw), P is any nonnegative probability row on paths (memory allowed; P << Q automatically since Q > 0; same initial value by construction). entropy_energy_estimate is eq:supp-entropy-energy: E_P sum_j E_j <= sum_j e_j + eps [sum_j log(1/p_{*,j}) + D_KL(P\|\|Q)] under the hypothesis that at each stage j and source x some proposal y has E_j(x,y) <= e_j and p_j(x,y) >= p_{*,j} > 0 (weaker than the paper's 'every proposal weight >= p_*'; nonnegativity of E is not needed). Instead of iterating the chain rule over prefixes, the proof uses the exact identity finiteKL_acceptedPathLaw: eps D(P\|\|Q) = eps D(P\|\|P_ref) + E_P E + eps E_P sum_j log Z_j(x_j) with P_ref = markovLaw x0 p the reference product law (markovLaw_sum_one), Gibbs' inequality D(P\|\|P_ref) >= 0, and the candidate bound Z_j(x) >= p_* exp(-e_j/eps). Disclosed scoping: reference rows and costs are functions of the current state (Markov Q), as in the paper's product-of-rows law. |
| `lem:supp-two-edge-schur` | lemma | Two-edge Schur identity | [`RenewalGeometry.two_edge_schur`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_completed_square`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_minimizer_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_blocked_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_eq_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.twoEdgeSchur_determinant`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.edgeQuadratic`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.schurMinimizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/DiscreteAnalysis/TwoEdgeSchurBlocking.lean) | Fully proved in the paper's generality. edgeQuadratic p q r x y = p x^2 - 2 q x y + r y^2; for p_i,q_i,r_i > 0 with d_i = p_i r_i - q_i^2 >= 0: (i) the minimizer z_* = (q_1 x + q_2 y)/(r_1+p_2) is positive on positive endpoints (twoEdgeSchur_minimizer_pos); (ii) the exact completed square E_1(x,z)+E_2(z,y) = E'(x,y) + (r_1+p_2)(z - z_*)^2 with the blocked coefficients p',q',r' of eq:supp-schur-coefficients (twoEdgeSchur_completed_square), hence E' is the minimum in z (twoEdgeSchur_blocked_le) attained exactly at z_* (twoEdgeSchur_eq_iff); (iii) the boxed determinant identity p'r' - q'^2 = (r_2 d_1 + p_1 d_2)/(r_1+p_2) (twoEdgeSchur_determinant, eq:supp-schur-determinant). two_edge_schur packages all clauses plus the byproduct p'r'-q'^2 >= 0. The identities only need r_1 + p_2 != 0; the d_i >= 0 hypotheses are carried for faithfulness. |

| Proved in the paper's generality. hyperbolicCost is eq:main-hyperbolic-cost (coth = cosh/sinh, csch = 1/sinh). For μ > 0, \|C\| ≤ μ (i.e. \|ρ\| ≤ 1, ρ = C/μ) and θ > 0: hyperbolicCost_sub_optimizer gives E(x,y) - E(x, m_θ x) = (μ coth θ + C)(y - m_θ x)^2 with m_θ x = r_θ x, r_θ = (cosh θ + ρ sinh θ)^{-1}, hence optimizerMap_isMin / optimizerMap_unique (unique minimizer over all real y, in particular over y > 0) and optimizerMap_pos (m_θ x > 0 for x > 0): eq:main-optimizer-map. optimizerRatio_inv_add_sub is eq:main-optimizer-defect for all θ, φ, ρ. optimizerMap_comp_iff: for one positive pair (θ, φ) and one x > 0, m_{θ+φ} x = m_φ (m_θ x) iff Δ_rel = 0; relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_comp packages both the every-positive-pair and the one-pair-at-one-x equivalences; relativeDefect_eq_zero_iff_mul_eq_sq identifies Δ_rel = 0 with AB = C^2 for μ = sqrt(AB), A, B > 0; optimizerRatio_of_relativeDefect_eq_zero gives ρ = ±1 and r_θ = exp(-ρ θ) on the matched branch. | `lem:supp-executed-proposal-floor` | lemma | A finite execution certificate for the proposal floor | [`RenewalGeometry.executed_proposal_floor`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.proposal_floor_arith`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.survivalProduct_telescope`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.survivalProduct`](../../RenewalGeometry/Action/ExecutedProposalFloor.lean) | Proved on an abstract probability space (MeasureTheory.Measure with IsProbabilityMeasure), bins 0..J-1 (the paper's 1..J shifted). The launch race is modelled by the decreasing no-earlier-launch events N l (N 0 = univ) and the predictor-marked first-launch events P l, a subset of N l \ N (l+1) (so they are automatically pairwise disjoint); the conditional probabilities 'given no earlier launch' are rendered in product form mu(N l \ N(l+1)) = a_l mu(N l), mu(P l) = f_l mu(N l), and the conditional execution floor as mu(G cap P l) >= q_* mu(P l). Under f_l >= rho_* a_l, -log S_J >= A_* > 0, 0 < rho_* <= 1, q_* > 0, executed_proposal_floor gives mu(G) >= q_* rho_* (1 - e^{-A_*}) (eq:supp-executed-proposal-floor). The proof derives mu(N l) = S_l by induction, sums the disjoint events G cap P l (measure_biUnion_finset), and applies the arithmetic certificate proposal_floor_arith (telescoping survivalProduct_telescope: sum S_l a_l = 1 - S_J, then S_J <= e^{-A_*} via exp_log). Disclosed: the hypotheses A_* > 0 and rho_* <= 1 are carried but not needed (the bound holds without them); the events are assumed measurable. |

Missing: the identification of the limit with eq:supp-einstein-insertion (thm:supp-renewal-palatini) and the mean-square-to-a.s. subsequence extraction of prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity, both entering as hypotheses. Unchanged this pass: the corollary's own argument is complete; it stays an interface by the corollary policy because its parent thm:supp-renewal-palatini (Lorentz commutant classification End_{SO+(1,3)}(Lambda^2 R^{1,3}) = span{I, *}, Palatini torsion vanishing with uniform Cartan inverse, weak-strong L^2 passage of coframe bivectors and curvature, first Bianchi removal of the Holst variation) and the almost-sure subsequence extraction of prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity are not formalized anywhere in the tree. | hard | Not attempted this pass (judged non-convergent within budget): the parent thm:supp-renewal-palatini is a large analytic theorem needing infrastructure the library lacks (represented connections and curvature on the common cylinder, the weak-strong Palatini lemma lem:supp-weak-strong-palatini, the Lorentz bivector commutant classification, thm:supp-palatini-torsion). A cheaper partial step that would remove one hypothesis of vacuum_equation_almost_surely is prop:supp-physical-test-stationarity's subsequence clause (mean-square convergence to 0 on a countable core => a deterministic subsequence with a.s. convergence for all tests: Chebyshev + Borel-Cantelli via MeasureTheory.tendsto_ae_of_summable or a diagonal argument), but the corollary remains an interface until the Palatini handoff (hrep) is proved. |

taggered.transport_lam_increment_nonneg`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.transport_nearest_neighbour`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.staggeredMomentum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.transport`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.GowdyStaggered.IsMidpointStep`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/GowdyStaggeredMomentumExact.lean) | Exact clauses proved on the periodic grid ZMod N with spacing l: the Gowdy state (U,P,Q,t per site, background lambda), the source flow eq:supp-gowdy-source-flow (full (U,P,Q,t) system), the implicit-midpoint relation Y = X + sigma F((X+Y)/2), and the characteristic transport map eq:supp-gowdy-transport (w+ -> S+ w+, w- -> S- w-, lambda -> lambda + (l/2)[(I+S+)g+ + (I+S-)g-], P,Q fixed) are encoded; proved: grad J . U' = 0 along the source flow, the quadratic endpoint identity J(v)-J(u) = grad J((u+v)/2).(v-u), hence every implicit-midpoint source step preserves J exactly; the transport identity Delta J = (1/2)[(S+ - I)g+ - (S- - I)g-] and D+ Delta lambda = 2 A+ Delta J (eq:supp-gowdy-transport-identity); the boxed K_l = D+ lambda - 2 A+ J(U) is preserved exactly by transport, by source steps, and by the source-half/transport/source-half composition (any source half-duration sigma, in particular the synchronized h = l, sigma = l/2); the lambda increment is (l/2) times a sum of squared characteristic amplitudes hence nonnegative for l >= 0; and the update at site j depends only on sites j-1, j, j+1 (nearest neighbour). Missing: the second-order consistency clause on bounded smooth reference charts with t >= t_0 > 0 (Taylor remainders of the trapezoid rule for the translated squares and of the implicit midpoint step for the smooth source, symmetric composition). Wave 3 (GowdySplittingConsistencyExact.lean): the two analytic ingredients of the consistency clause are now proved. (a) transport_lam_trapezoid_error: when the characteristic squares at sites j, j+1 (resp. j-1, j) sample C^2 functions g+ (resp. g-) at x, x+l (resp. x-l, x) with \|g''\| <= zeta+-, the background increment lambda+_j - lambda_j of transport differs from int_x^{x+l} g+ + int_{x-l}^x g- by at most l^3 (zeta+ + zeta-)/12 (Mathlib trapezoidal_error_le_of_c2). (b) sourceStep_residual: a C^3 trajectory Z of the local source flow (Z' = localField Z in the vector encoding LocalState.toVec, isMidpointStep_iff_toVec restates IsMidpointStep in vector form) on a chart K where the vector source field localFieldVec is L-Lipschitz satisfies the implicit-midpoint relation of duration sigma = b - a up to a residual of norm <= sigma^3 (M3/24 + L M2/8), M2, M3 bounding the second and third derivatives of the trajectory on [a,b]; via the generic vector-valued fencing bounds symmetric_difference_bound (\|\|Y(c+s)+Y(c-s)-2Y(c)\|\| <= M s^2), midpoint_rule_bound (\|\|X(b)-X(a)-(b-a)X'((a+b)/2)\|\| <= M3 (b-a)^3/24) and implicit_midpoint_residual. Still missing for 'proved': the L-Lipschitz property of the Gowdy source field on the concrete chart {t >= t_0 > 0, \|U\|,\|P\|,\|Q\| bounded} is taken as the chart hypothesis (it holds since the field is C^1 there, but the explicit constant is not derived), and the symmetric-composition (Strang) local error of the full source-half/transport/source-half step against the continuum Gowdy system is not assembled because the continuum system and its exact solution are not encoded in Lean. Wave 4 (new file Gravity/GowdySourceFieldLipschitz.lean, compiled, standard axioms): item (1) of the previous plan is done. gowdyChart t0 R = {t >= t0, \|u_i\| <= R, \|P\| <= R} is the bounded smooth reference chart (Q is unconstrained since it enters only through dQ/ds), gowdyLipschitz t0 R = 1/(2t0) + R/(2t0^2) + 4R/sqrt t0 + R^2/(t0 sqrt t0) + e^R(R+1)/sqrt t0 + R e^R/(2 t0 sqrt t0) is an explicit Lipschitz constant, localFieldVec_lipschitz_on_chart proves \|\|F x - F y\|\| <= gowdyLipschitz t0 R \|\|x - y\|\| on the chart for the vector source field (sup norms; elementary bounds \|1/(2t)-1/(2t')\| <= \|t-t'\|/(2t0^2), \|1/sqrt t - 1/sqrt t'\| <= \|t-t'\|/(2 t0 sqrt t0), \|e^{-P}-e^{-P'}\| <= e^R\|P-P'\|), and sourceStep_residual_chart is sourceStep_residual with hLip discharged: a C^3 trajectory of the local source flow with midpoints in the chart satisfies the implicit-midpoint relation of duration sigma up to sigma^3 (M3/24 + gowdyLipschitz t0 R * M2/8). STILL MISSING for 'proved' (difficulty hard): the symmetric-composition (Strang) local error of the full source-half/transport/source-half step against the continuum Gowdy characteristic system, which requires the continuum system and its smooth solution to be encoded and a second-order Taylor expansion of the split flows with uniform remainders; Mathlib has no regularity-of-flows theory (only Picard-Lindelof existence), so the standard Strang local-error theorem (C^2 dependence of the flows on the step and the Lie-commutator expansion) has no infrastructure to build on. | hard | (a) Encode the continuum Gowdy system in characteristic form on the chart: fields w+-(t,x) (in R^2), P, Q, lambda with d_t w+- = +-d_x w+- + (source part of eq:supp-gowdy-source-flow transformed to w+-), d_t P = u1/sqrt t, d_t Q = e^{-P} u3/sqrt t, d_t lambda = g+ + g-, and a C^3 solution X_* sampled on the grid (S_l X_*(t_j)). (b) Prove exact characteristic translation for the pure advection part (d/ds w(t+s, x-+s) = 0 via HasFDerivAt on R^2; ~40 lines) so that transport is the exact flow of the advection field with h = l. (c) The hard core: a Strang local-error theorem. Either (c1) formalize the general statement 'for x' = A(x) + B(x) with C^2 fields on a convex chart, \|\|phi_B^{h/2} o phi_A^h o phi_B^{h/2}(x) - phi^h(x)\|\| <= C h^3' — this needs existence, uniqueness and C^2 dependence on time of the flows of A, B, A+B (Mathlib: ODE_solution_unique and Picard-Lindelof exist, but no smooth dependence; one must either add a Gronwall-based Taylor argument with the fencing lemmas of GowdySplittingConsistencyExact applied to the composed trajectories, assuming the intermediate half-step trajectories exist as C^3 paths — a heavily conditional statement) — or (c2) avoid flows: state second-order consistency as the local truncation error of the one-step map Phi_l applied to the sampled exact solution, and prove \|\|S_l X_*(t_{j+1}) - Phi_l(S_l X_*(t_j))\|\| <= C l^3 directly by Taylor-expanding X_* in t to second order, using that the transport part is exact (b), that the two implicit-midpoint half-steps agree with the exact source flow to O(l^3) (sourceStep_residual_chart) and that the commutator [source, advection] contributes O(l^3) after the symmetric arrangement; this still requires an implicit-function argument to compare the implicit midpoint map (defined by a fixed-point relation, IsMidpointStep) with the explicit sampled data, i.e. a Lipschitz-invertibility lemma for the implicit map for small l (contraction, gowdyLipschitz t0 R * l/2 < 1). Estimated several days; the missing infrastructure is smooth dependence / composition of flows, not available in Mathlib. |

| `thm:supp-finite-defects` | theorem | First-order consistency of the exact-link regulator | [`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.timeTransport_metric_unitary`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.torsionCellRemainder_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.curvatureCellRemainder_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.palatiniCellRemainder_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.normalizedTorsionDefect_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.explicit_geometric_defects_summable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RelationalDeSitterBranch.diagonalMesh_summable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Gravity/RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean) | Partial: for the homogeneous de Sitter branch Lean proves exact metric-unitary time-line transport, explicit scalar Taylor-remainder models of the torsion, face-curvature and midpoint Palatini cell defects with area-normalized bounds C_H h_n (C_H depending on H only) on the diagonal mesh h_n = 2^{-n^2}, and their summability. Missing: the actual nonabelian-Stokes face holonomy log U_{dp} + \|p\| Omega, the covariant developed-edge torsion defect, the Palatini first-variation quadrature error over O(h^{-4}) cells with a slab constant C_T, the sup over faces and C^1 tests, the flat-regulator zero statement, and the T_n = n exhaustion with C_{T_n} <= C e^{cn}. | hard | The existing Lean (RelationalDeSitterBranch.lean torsionCellRemainder_le, curvatureCellRemainder_le, palatiniCellRemainder_le, explicit_geometric_defects_summable) only bounds scalar Taylor-remainder models. The real statement needs the path-ordered exact link U_e = P exp(-int omega) (no product integral / nonabelian Stokes in Mathlib), the face holonomy expansion log U_dp = -\|p\| Omega + O(h^3), the developed-edge torsion defect, the midpoint quadrature error of the Palatini first variation over O(h^{-4}) cells with a C^1 test-field sup, and the exponential growth C_{T_n} <= C e^{cn} of derivative bounds on [-n,n]. Only the last summability step (Summable of C e^{cn} 2^{-n^2}, cf. summable_of_defect_le_mesh) is easy. Infrastructure-level ODE/holonomy analysis. |

The C¹-consistency transfer (eliminated map z_h(x_h) is C¹ consistent when F_h and its derivatives are) is the implicit-function derivative formula D z = -(D_zF)^{-1} D_xF with the Neumann bound for (D_zF)^{-1} = (I - (I - A D_zF))^{-1} A (Analysis/ResolventNeumannSeries.lean); encode ass:supp-initial-consistency as a hypothesis structure rather than proving the composed finite phase-difference/Legendre chain. |
