| `thm:supp-chronological-thomson` | theorem | Chronological source-shorted Thomson correction | medium | Finite linear algebra on edge arrays indexed by a finite set E ⊆ X × Y with R > 0: define A, B (row/column sums), the R^{-1} inner product, P_s = I - Aᵀ diag(α)^{-1} A (orthogonal projection onto ker A in the R^{-1} geometry), L = B P_s Bᵀ; prove eq:supp-thomson-L by expanding (diag ρ = B R-weights, Qᵀ diag α Q from A) and the variance identity eq:supp-thomson-variance (Σ_x α(x) Var_{Q(x,·)}(u) = Σ α u² weighted minus squares of conditional means) which gives L ⪰ 0; solvability Ah = 0, Bh = d ⇔ d ∈ Ran L via the projection; the minimum-norm solution h_* = R(u(y) - (Qu)(x)) with u = L^† d (use Analysis/HermitianMoorePenroseInverse.lean: exists_hermitian_moorePenroseInverse for the symmetric PSD L) and ‖h_*‖²_{R^{-1}} = ⟨d, L^† d⟩; the leverage clause max\|u(y) - (Qu)(x)\| ≤ 1/2 ⇒ R + h_* ≥ 0 with marginals α, β is pointwise. Build in Action/ or MarkovChains/ (marginal bookkeeping as in Certificates/MarginalsOrientation.lean). |
| `lem:supp-same-cylinder-transfer` | lemma | Variance-sensitive source-preserving transfer | easy | Finite-sum Cauchy-Schwarz in a new file RenewalGeometry/Action/SameCylinderTransferExact.lean: on a finite product index set with a positive reference coupling R, \|\|h\|\|_{R^{-1}}^2 = sum h^2/R and V_R(c) = sum R (c - m)^2 with m = E_R c; if the marginal map A kills h (row and column sums of h vanish, in particular sum h = 0) then sum h c = sum h (c - m) and \|sum h (c - m)\| <= \|\|h\|\|_{R^{-1}} V_R(c)^{1/2} (Finset.inner_mul_le_norm_mul_norm / Real.sum_mul_le_sqrt_mul_sqrt); the Thomson clause is the instance h = h_*, E_occ = \|\|h_*\|\|^2_{R^{-1}}; the last clause V_R(c) <= B m for 0 <= c <= B is sum R (c - m)^2 = sum R c^2 - m^2 <= B m. |
| `prop:supp-same-cylinder-link-budget` | proposition | Constructive metric jets imply the literal-link budget | hard | Requires the constructive writer's metric-jet bounds (hard PDE layer) and the spinless Cartan reconstruction of A_h, A_{0,h}, E_h, F_h from metric jets with grid Sobolev algebra estimates (products in H_h^s, s ≥ 3); nothing short of the full 3+1 infrastructure gives the uniform budget. |
| `lem:supp-exact-rational-detuning` | lemma | Finite rational detuning | hard | Requires quantitative spectral information on the cosine product-defect Jacobi matrix D_h (simple spectrum, reciprocal-eigenvalue sum O(h^{-3}), spectral gaps versus 4h^6) and a measure argument on the ratio set, then a tensor-product eigenbasis argument for the three squared components; the paper proof is itself sketchy on the h^{-3} bound. Nothing on Jacobi/tridiagonal spectra exists (Jacobi hits are determinant-derivative files). Finite-dimensional but a research-level quantitative spectral estimate; the existential 'finite algebraic search' is not a usable Lean statement without the margin proof. |
| `thm:supp-exact-active-regular` | theorem | All-cutoff regular active source | hard | Combines the exact preparation chart (hard, thm:supp-action-prepared-chart), the rational detuning margin (hard) and the transverse block (medium) into an h^16 Gram floor for the complete leading active source of the exact action, which is not defined in Lean. Only the kernel description constant lapse plus curl-free shifts would be finite algebra once the source matrix is defined. |
| `thm:supp-exact-neutral-preparation` | theorem | Exact source-neutral finite preparation | hard | Needs the weak quadratic target of the full retained action on the balanced family (polarization invariants), the uniform range inverse and four-mean calibration (hard chain), a concrete three-site signed-Gram determinant computed from the exact action, and the literal temporal/spatial link-curvature formulas. None of the exact-action objects exist; the vanishing of the two polarization invariants (equal norms, orthogonality of T_i and P_i) is an easy Fin 3 matrix fact but is a small fraction of the statement. |

Without the exact-action pipeline this cannot be stated faithfully; a scoped abstract version (compression of a positive metric with uniformly invertible coefficient map has bounds inherited from that map) would be medium. |

Research-level. |
| `thm:supp-exact-upstream-deformation` | theorem | The fixed-first-jet tangency obstruction is deformable | hard | The conclusion is a nonzero rational number obtained by polarizing the tangency functional along an exact lower-incidence tangent (Z_2, m_2) of the three-site exact action; in principle a decide/norm_num computation, but it presupposes Lean definitions of the lower-incidence map L(Y,l), the first Poisson matrix and the multiplier system for the exact finite action, none of which exist. Hard until the exact-action objects (also needed by thm:supp-exact-initial-layer) are encoded. |

After constructing them with exact rational entries, rank 24 could be certified by exhibiting an invertible 24 x 24 minor and a 5-dimensional kernel basis (Matrix.rank via Matrix.rank_eq_finrank_range and explicit kernel vectors) without native_decide, but the construction is the hard part. |

Infrastructure-level. |

| `prop:supp-exact-boundary-residual` | proposition | A posteriori boundary residual estimate | hard | An a posteriori error bound for the two-point boundary contraction of thm:main-exact-boundary: with the boundary-value solution operator (norm M_D on the boundary data, C_G(1+S) on the residual) and contraction constant q, \|\|w~ - w_*\|\| <= (M_D(\|d_-\|+\|d_+\|) + C_G(1+S)\|\|r\|\|)/(1-q). | `lem:supp-exact-graph-criterion` | lemma | A quantitative forward invariant-graph criterion | hard | Lyapunov–Perron construction of a C³ forward-invariant graph u = h(n) for a partially hyperbolic ODE with global Lipschitz nonlinearity and the gap condition eq:supp-exact-graph-contraction (fixed point in exponentially weighted spaces, then differentiability through order three by the fibre-contraction theorem). Mathlib has ODE existence/uniqueness (Picard–Lindelöf, Gronwall) but no weighted-space Lyapunov–Perron or fibre-contraction machinery; a multi-week infrastructure project. |

Slow part: the scalar factor eq:supp-exact-slow-normal-factor gives e^{(λ_+ - γ/4)τ}\|ω(0)\| ≤ \|ω(τ)\| ≤ e^{(λ_+ + γ/4)τ}\|ω(0)\| by the scalar Gronwall/integrating-factor lemma (Mathlib norm_le_gronwallBound_of_norm_deriv_right_le) — that half is medium once ass:supp-exact-normal is encoded as hypotheses. |

The abstract ODE lower bound could be proved in a new Gravity file (build on Gravity/CurvatureEnergyPropagationExact.lean for the Gronwall layer, with a lower-bound variant), but the statement is about the actual charts of ass:supp-exact-normal, which are undefined; a faithful record needs at least the assumption structure. After construction, rank 26 with cokernel H is a finite exact-arithmetic certificate (explicit 26 x 26 invertible minor plus kernel basis). |

Identification with the actual Phi_0, Phi_1, C_sl of the exact action is not possible yet (undefined objects). |

Encode 𝓛_0, f_0, 𝒦_0, p_g as data and the expansion as hypotheses; even then the derivation of eq:supp-exact-initial-acceleration-defect needs second-order differentiation of the composed source in a — medium after encoding, hard overall. |
| `cor:supp-exact-initial-repair` | corollary | Sufficient acceleration certificate | hard | Corollary of thm:supp-exact-initial-gate (unformalized, hard): if the limit in eq:supp-exact-initial-acceleration-defect is o(a^{-1}) times a, the two-row defect vanishes. | `prop:supp-exact-initial-counterexample` | proposition | Analytic preparation does not imply the two-row cancellation | medium | Explicit ODE: the Euler–Lagrange equations of eq:supp-exact-counterexample-L are α' = 0 (so α ≡ a), a² z'' = -(z - a) (harmonic oscillator of frequency 1/a) and λ' = a (z')² + (z - a); with z(0) - a = O(a) and z'(0) bounded, z = a + A cos(t/a) + B sin(t/a) with A, B = O(a) has bounded z', z'' on every fixed interval and λ' bounded, giving analytic-in-a initial records (Real.cos/sin, ring identities). The 'analogue of the upper homological row' must be encoded from the appendix (the O(a^{-1}) coefficient of the multiplier row); state it as an explicit nonvanishing coefficient of this concrete family. New file in Gravity/ using Mathlib's ODE solution formulas (HasDerivAt computations) — days of concrete calculus, no missing infrastructure. |

Use Matrix.fromBlocks_mulVec, Matrix.mul_nonsing_inv and Algebra/PsdBlockSchurExact.lean for the Schur complement lemmas; one day. |

The second layer (equality with the initial-data expression eq:supp-finite-acc-initial-verdict for families satisfying thm:supp-exact-initial-gate, and the elimination of prop:supp-finite-acc-schur) inherits the unformalized exact-action objects; hard as a whole. |

A C¹ version with the derivative formula is medium; the analytic claim is missing infrastructure. |

Research-level in the present state of the library. |

Research-level. |
| `lem:supp-exact-ward-orders` | lemma | Constant-shift derivative orders | hard | Needs the constant-shift compatibility rows ℬ_c of the exact action as analytic functions of (X, λ) with their gauge structure (the rows vanish identically at X = 0 and are quadratic in X for the λ-derivative), then Landau-order estimates along the analytic family uniformly on compact parameter sets; requires the exact finite action (undefined in Lean) and multi-parameter O(a^k) calculus. Could become medium if the rows are encoded as explicit polynomials in (X, λ) with the vanishing structure as hypotheses. |
| `thm:supp-exact-harmonic-q4-zero` | theorem | Base-uniform harmonic quartic annihilation | hard | A vanishing identity for the quartic constant-row coefficient of the N = 3 exact action on harmonic weak directions; requires the explicit finite action and its fourth-order Taylor coefficient (undefined in Lean). Once the action is a Lean polynomial, the identity is finite algebra (possibly heavy, 3 x 3 grid with 108 rows); hard until then. |
| `thm:supp-exact-rank-two-factorization` | theorem | Canonical rank-two factorization | medium | Encode the record-preserving branch data: the slope map 𝒬_can : ℝ³ → ℝ³, the constant q_{0,can}, the 3×2 matrix 𝒞 (built from 𝒞_g of rank ≤ 2) and the quadratic map ℛ : ℝ³ → ℝ²; the factorization eq:supp-exact-rank-two-factorization follows from prop:supp-exact-original-slope-jets by collecting the terms that factor through the two g-rows — this derivation is the real content and needs those reduced functions. Given the factorization, D𝒬_can(c) = -𝒞 Dℛ(c) is the chain rule and rank D𝒬 ≤ rank 𝒞 ≤ 2 is LinearMap.rank_comp_le_left / Matrix.rank_mul_le. Days once the slope map is defined. |
| `cor:supp-exact-slope-cokernel` | corollary | Cokernel obstruction | easy | Abstract corollary of the factorization eq:supp-exact-rank-two-factorization (take it as hypothesis): if Q_can(c) = q_0 + C_g w(c) for some map w (the linear and quadratic harmonic-slope coefficients factor through C_g), then for lambda with C_g^T lambda = 0, lambda . Q_can(c) = lambda . q_0 (Matrix.dotProduct_mulVec / vecMul_transpose), and if q_0 is not in Ran C_g there is such a lambda with lambda . q_0 != 0 (orthogonal complement of the range in finite dimension: Submodule.orthogonal, or LinearMap.range_eq_top via annihilator), hence Q_can(c) != 0 for every c. New small file in RenewalGeometry/Algebra/. |
| `thm:supp-exact-two-equation-slope` | theorem | Two-equation physical slope reduction | hard | The equivalence 𝒬_can(c) = 0 ⇔ ℛ(c) = y_0 under eq:supp-exact-rank-two-membership (q_{0,can} = 𝒞 y_0 with 𝒞 injective of rank two) is immediate linear algebra from the factorization of thm:supp-exact-rank-two-factorization (easy once that is encoded). The local one-dimensional real-analytic submanifold structure of {ℛ = y_0} ⊂ ℝ³ at a root where Dℛ has rank two needs the analytic submersion/implicit-function theorem; Mathlib has smooth submersion-level-set results only partially and no analytic submanifolds — missing infrastructure. |
| `prop:supp-exact-slope-contraction` | proposition | Two-dimensional contraction certificate | medium | New file RenewalGeometry/Analysis/QuadraticNewtonCertificate.lean: for F(y+q) = F(y) + J q + B[q,q] exactly (a quadratic polynomial map on a finite-dimensional normed space; R^2 in the paper) and an invertible M, the map T(q) = q - M F(y+q) satisfies \|\|T q - T q'\|\| <= (theta + beta r) \|\|q - q'\|\| on the closed r-ball (mean value bound with \|\|I - MJ\|\| = theta, \|\|2 M B\|\| = beta) and \|\|T 0\|\| = eta, so q_* < 1 and eta + q_* r < r give a self-map contraction; Banach fixed point (ContractingWith.exists_fixedPoint' / Metric.closedBall completeness) gives the unique zero, interior since \|\|q\|\| <= eta + q_* r < r; build on the contraction pattern of Action/CriticalShadowingExact.lean. The continuation clause (slice roots continue to a local root curve when the 2 x 2 derivative stays invertible) is Mathlib's implicit function theorem for C^1 maps (HasStrictFDerivAt.implicitFunction); analyticity of the root curve is not available in Mathlib and should be disclosed. |
| `thm:supp-finite-range-test` | theorem | All-rank range test and sharp affine obstruction | medium | Finite linear algebra in ℝ³ with C_* : Matrix (Fin 3) (Fin 2) ℝ: replace C_* C_*^† by the orthogonal projection onto the column span (Submodule.starProjection / orthogonalProjection in EuclideanSpace ℝ (Fin 3)), prove q ∈ Ran C ⇔ rank (C \| q) = rank C (Matrix.rank via LinearMap.range of the augmented matrix, Submodule.span_insert) ⇔ e_* = 0; the floor ‖𝒬_can(c)‖ ≥ ‖e_*‖ is the distance from q_* - 𝒞ℛ(c) ∈ q_* - Ran C to 0 being ≥ the orthogonal residual (take 𝒬_can(c) = q_* - C_* ℛ(c) as hypothesis from thm:supp-exact-rank-two-factorization); for rank two use crossProduct c_1 c_2 as a normal vector (cross_dot_self lemmas) and \|nᵀq\|/‖n‖ = \|det(c_1,c_2,q)\| / ‖c_1 × c_2‖ via triple_product_eq_det; rank one: u × q = 0 ⇔ q ∈ span u (cross product vanishing iff parallel, small lemma), rank zero trivial. Days of work in a new Algebra/ or Gravity/ file. |
| `prop:supp-finite-range-pfaffian` | proposition | One bordered Pfaffian for the range obstruction | hard | Requires the Pfaffian of a skew-symmetric matrix and its bordered-matrix expansion (Pf B(K,R,d) as a linear form in d built from the 2 x 2 Pfaffian minors of the rank-(2m-2) kernel), infrastructure absent from Mathlib (Matrix.det exists, Pfaffian does not). A determinant reformulation (det of the bordered (2m+3) x (2m+3) skew matrix = Pf^2, and the rank statements at/away from membership) could be attempted with Matrix.det and Matrix.rank on the kernel decomposition, but defining and proving properties of the Pfaffian first is a medium-to-hard library task; the 30 x 30 instance for K = K_g additionally needs the (undefined) K_g. |
| `prop:supp-gowdy-residual-control` | proposition | Residual and constraint control | hard | The discrete Gronwall iteration itself (Lipschitz 1+Ch, residual sum) is medium and could be a standalone lemma (no discrete Gronwall exists in the library), but the statement needs the smooth Gowdy reference solution X_*, the grid sampling S_l, the C^r_l norms with inverse estimates, and the O(h^3) consistency of the split map against the Gowdy PDE, i.e. Taylor expansions of a PDE solution in two variables; PDE reference objects are absent from Mathlib/library. The exact constraint-increment formula is finite algebra given thm:supp-gowdy-momentum. |
| `thm:supp-gowdy-full-curvature` | theorem | Pathwise full-curvature control on the Gowdy alphabet | hard | Requires cubic Hermite reconstruction on a 2D grid with W^{1,inf} and second-jet remainder estimates, the reconstructed Gowdy metric's Christoffel and Riemann tensors as functions of a piecewise-polynomial C^1 metric, and L^inf/L^2 curvature bounds; no Hermite interpolation, no continuum curvature of a general metric (Gravity/CoordinateCurvature.lean only handles diagonal FLRW jets). Infrastructure-level. |
| `lem:supp-flat-symbol` | lemma | Fourier symbol and flat generator limit | hard | As stated it includes strong-resolvent and semigroup convergence of L_h to (1/2) Delta on L^2(T^3), which needs the continuum torus Laplacian as an unbounded operator and the varying-Hilbert Mosco machinery (OperatorLimits/VaryingHilbertMosco.lean, MoscoResolvent.lean exist but no continuum Laplacian on T^3 exists in Mathlib); that clause is infrastructure-level. The finite part is medium and should be split off: define the generator on (ZMod d)^3 from A3PeriodicGraphSamplingExact.lean (mass/conductance/rootStep) and a3Roots, show e_k is an eigenvector with lambda_h(k) = (1/(4h^2)) sum_{a=1}^6 [1 - cos(2 pi h <r_a,k>)] by pairing +-alpha (Real.cos_neg), then the pointwise limit 2 pi^2 \|k\|^2 from (1-cos x)/x^2 -> 1/2 (Mathlib Real.one_sub_cos or tendsto via Taylor) and the tight-frame identity Gravity/RelationalFlatVacuum.lean:relational_flat_vacuum / A3FiniteDifferenceConsistencyExact.lean:tight_frame_energy (sum over 6 oriented roots = 4I). |
